| `fegg/MyApplication.php`                                        | `htdocs/_fegg/site/controller/Controller.php`                    |
| `code/lib/Modifire.php`                                         | `htdocs/_fegg/site/functions/modifier.php`                       |
| Other project libraries in `code/lib/`                          | `htdocs/_fegg/site/src/` (`Site\`)                               |
| Shared CMS screens in `code/application/cms/` (Index, Login, Logout, Media, Image, Multi, Migrate, Register) | `htdocs/_fegg/core/controller/` (use the new version) |
| Everything else in `code/application/`                          | `htdocs/_fegg/site/controller/` (`cms/` becomes `Cms/`)          |
| Shared CMS templates in `code/template/cms/`                    | `htdocs/_fegg/core/template/cms/` (use the new version)          |
| Everything else in `code/template/`                             | `htdocs/_fegg/site/template/`                                    |
| `code/config/`, `code/config/database/` (project tables)        | `htdocs/_fegg/site/config/`, `htdocs/_fegg/site/config/database/` |
| `code/data/`                                                    | `htdocs/_fegg/site/data/`                                        |
| `htdocs/index.php`, `fegg-cli`                                  | Recreate from `core/dist/index.php.dist` and `core/dist/fegg-cli.dist` |
| `htdocs/.htaccess`                                              | Add the Fegg rules from `core/dist/htaccess.dist`                |

Then:

1. Copy `htdocs/_fegg/core/`, `htdocs/_fegg/.htaccess` and `htdocs/cms/` from the Fegg 5 skeleton, and run `npm run build:scss`.
2. Recreate the entry files. If the project changed the paths in `index.php`, set the three constants (`FEGG_HTML_DIR`, `FEGG_CORE_DIR`, `FEGG_SITE_DIR`) to match (see [Directory structure](./cont.mdx#entry-files-and-layout)).
3. Add the `FilesMatch` block and the `RewriteRule (^|/)_fegg(/|$) - [F,L]` line from `htaccess.dist` to `htdocs/.htaccess`.
4. Move the project files with `git mv` (keeps the history).
5. Carry over the changed values of the old `fegg/settings.php` (`developer_ip`, `run_mode`, `maintenance_page_url`, Basic authentication, languages) to `site/config/settings.php`. Copy `site/config/env.php.dist` to `site/config/env.php` on each development or staging server and set `'FEGG_ENV' => 'development'`: without it the site runs as production and shows no error details (see [Settings](./config.mdx#environments-and-secrets)).
6. Create `site/config/cms_menu.php` with one block per post type that was in the old CMS menu.
7. Replace the removed constants until `grep -rn "FEGG_DIR\|FEGG_CODE_DIR" htdocs/_fegg/site` finds nothing:

   | v3 / v4                              | Fegg 5                                 |
   | :----------------------------------- | :------------------------------------- |
   | `FEGG_CODE_DIR . '/config/...'`      | `FEGG_SITE_DIR . '/config/...'`        |
   | `FEGG_CODE_DIR . '/data/...'`        | `FEGG_SITE_DIR . '/data/...'`          |
   | `FEGG_CODE_DIR . '/application/...'` | `FEGG_SITE_DIR . '/controller/...'`    |
   | `FEGG_CODE_DIR . '/lib/...'`         | A namespaced class in `site/src/`      |
   | `FEGG_DIR . '/...'`                  | Not needed (autoloaded)                |

At this point the site should work again. Old-style controllers (no namespace, `extends MyApplication`, `getClass()`) still run.

## 3. Move core changes into overrides in `site/`

Go through the list from step 1:

- Changed template of a shared CMS screen: put the changed file at the same path in `site/template/cms/`.
- Changed controller of a shared CMS screen: extend the core class (`class Media extends \Fegg\Controller\Cms\Media`) and write only the changed methods (see [Controller](../basic/controller.mdx#extending-a-core-controller)).
- Code added to `MyApplication`: move it to `__init()` of `site/controller/Controller.php`.
- Changed shared library: create a subclass in `site/src/` and use it.

When `./fegg-cli core:status` prints `No changes to core files.`, this step is done.

## 4. Move to namespaces

The Fegg 4 style keeps working through `Fegg\Legacy`, but every use writes `Fegg deprecated: ...` to the PHP error log, and the compatibility layer will be removed in Fegg 6. Use the log to fix the code step by step.

```php title="Fegg 4: code/application/cms/News.php"
<?php
class News extends MyApplication
{
    public function __init()
    {
        parent::__init();
        $this->_lh = $this->getClass('LH_FW', 'news');
        $this->_model = new Model(true, array('table' => 'cms_news', 'id' => 'news_id'));
    }
}
```

```php title="Fegg 5: htdocs/_fegg/site/controller/Cms/News.php"
<?php
namespace Site\Controller\Cms;

defined('FEGG_CORE_DIR') || exit;

use Fegg\Model;

class News extends Controller              // Site\Controller\Cms\Controller: the CMS base
{
    protected $_cmsSection = 'news';       // replaces new Helper('news') and the isAuth() check

    public function __init()
    {
        parent::__init();
        $this->_model = new Model(true, array('table' => 'cms_news', 'id' => 'news_id'));
    }

    public function update()
    {
        $this->requirePost();              // required: every action that changes data
        // ...
    }

    public function delete($id)
    {
        $this->requirePost();              // deletes are POST forms with the CSRF token
        // ...
    }
}
```

CMS screens extend `Site\Controller\Cms\Controller` (copy `site/controller/Cms/Controller.php` from the skeleton), not `Site\Controller\Controller`: it checks the login and the CSRF token of every POST. Delete links in copied CMS list templates become POST forms (see [Controller](../basic/controller.mdx#cms-screens)).

:::danger Required: `requirePost()` in every action that changes data

Fegg 4 had no such check, so copied CMS controllers do not have it. Add `$this->requirePost()` as the first line of every action that saves, deletes, sorts or otherwise writes (`update`, `create`, `delete` ...). The CSRF token is only checked on POST: an action without `requirePost()` still runs on a GET, so a link or an image on another site can change data while a CMS user is logged in. With it, a GET answers 405. Check every action with `grep -n "public function" htdocs/_fegg/site/controller/Cms/*.php`.

:::

| v3 / v4                                    | Fegg 5                                              |
| :----------------------------------------- | :-------------------------------------------------- |
| `MyApplication`                            | `Site\Controller\Controller` (public pages), `Site\Controller\Cms\Controller` (CMS screens) |
| `Application` / `CommandApplication`       | `Fegg\Application` / `Fegg\CommandApplication`      |
| `Model`, `ModelWhere`, `ModelOrder`, ...   | `Fegg\Model`, `Fegg\Model\Where`, `Fegg\Model\Order`, ... |
| `DB_pdo`                                   | `Fegg\Database\Pdo`                                 |
| `LH_FW`                                    | `Fegg\Cms\Helper`                                   |
| `Validation`                               | `Fegg\Validation`                                   |
| `ReCAPTCHA`, `Turnstile`                   | `Fegg\Captcha\ReCAPTCHA`, `Fegg\Captcha\Turnstile`  |
| `Tool/Date`, `Tool/File`, `Tool/Mail`, `Tool/OEmbed`, `Tool/RSS`, `Tool/Trim`, `Tool/Upload` | `Fegg\Tool\Date`, `Fegg\Tool\File`, ... |
| `$this->getClass('LH_FW', 'news')`         | `new \Fegg\Cms\Helper('news')`                      |
| `$this->getClass('Validation')`            | `new \Fegg\Validation()`                            |
| `$this->getClass('Tool/Upload')`           | `new \Fegg\Tool\Upload()`                           |
| `$this->getClass('MyLib/Foo')`             | `new \Site\MyLib\Foo()` in `site/src/MyLib/Foo.php` |

Inside a namespace, prefix PHP's own classes with `\` (`new \Exception`, `new \DateTime`, `\PDO::PARAM_INT`).

You are done when `grep -rn "getClass\|MyApplication\|extends Model\b" htdocs/_fegg/site` finds nothing and the error log has no `Fegg deprecated` entries.

## Behaviour changes to check

Fegg 5 fails loudly where Fegg 4 was silent. The full list is in the 5.0.0 section of `htdocs/_fegg/core/UPGRADE.md`. The ones most likely to affect an old project:

- **Database errors** throw `Fegg\Database\QueryException` (the message includes the SQL and its parameters) instead of printing and calling `exit`. Uncaught, it is a logged HTTP 500.
- **Missing templates**, `include` targets and `transclude` parents throw `Fegg\Template\TemplateException` (HTTP 500) instead of printing "Can't Read" into the page. `./fegg-cli template:lint` lists them all.
- **Template blocks are checked**: an unclosed block or an extra `{{ end }}` throws with the line number.
- **Sections** are passed to the parent template as closures. Code that called the global `section_<name>()` functions directly must be rewritten.
- `updateWhere()`, `deleteWhere()` and `eraseWhere()` with an **empty condition** throw instead of affecting every row. Pass `'1 = 1'` where every row was really meant.
- `Model::order()`, `join()` and the `table` / `id` / `visible` options accept **only plain names**. Move expressions to `orderRaw()`.
- `{{ options }}` escapes labels and values, and `{{ hidden }}` escapes values.
- **`OR` in conditions** is put in brackets before the model adds the visible flag and `valid = 1`. In Fegg 4, `where('a = ?', 1)->where('b = ?', 2, 'OR')` also returned unpublished and deleted rows matching `b`; such lists can get shorter.
- **Arrays reach modifiers**: `{{ $tags|feggImplode:', ' }}` now prints the joined tags (it printed nothing).
- **`where()` fragments with `OR`** are bracketed: `where('category = ?', 1)->where('a = ? OR b = ?', ...)` is `category = 1 AND (a = ? OR b = ?)`. Fegg 4 matched rows of every category.
- **Actions**: only a controller's own public methods can be called from a URL (see [Controller](../basic/controller.mdx#which-methods-are-actions)). An action defined in `MyApplication` or a base controller is a 404: write it in each controller that uses it. An action named like a method of `Fegg\Application` (`mail`, `in`, `redirect` ...) is a 404: rename it, or map the old URL with a route.
- **CMS saving actions** with `requirePost()` answer 405 to a GET (see above). Edit and delete forms in copied templates must use `method="post"`.
- **Basic authentication** with `basic_auth_scope = 1` is checked before the controller's `__init()` and action, so redirects, downloads and direct output are protected too.
- **CMS sessions** need the session's user to still exist in `cms_user`: deleting a user ends their sessions.
- **No more `exit`**: `displayPage()`, `redirect()`, `requirePost()` and the CMS login check throw `Fegg\Http\Halt`. A `catch (\Throwable $e)` around them must rethrow it (see [Controller](../basic/controller.mdx#ending-a-response)). Replace your own `exit` in controllers with `$this->halt()`.
- **Environment**: error details need a `developer_ip` address **and** `FEGG_ENV` other than `production` (`site/config/env.php`). `FEGG_TEST_ENVIRONMENT` no longer looks at the host name.
- **Sessions and cookies**: a session starts only when something is written to it. `setCookie()` takes SameSite as its own argument, and `unsetCookie()` really deletes the cookie (see [Parameters](../basic/parameters/index.mdx)).
- **Security headers** (`X-Frame-Options: SAMEORIGIN`, `nosniff`, `Referrer-Policy`) are sent with every response. A page meant to be framed by another site calls `$this->allowFraming()` (see [Security](../basic/security.mdx)).
- **CMS login**: logout is a POST. Copied CMS frame templates in `site/template/cms/` add the class `js-cms-logout` to the logout link. Run `fegg-cli migration` for the login throttling table `cms_login_attempt`. `/cms/login/create/...` is gone: use `fegg-cli user:create` (see [fegg-cli](./cli.mdx#cms-users)).
- **Uploads** check the content against the extension; add the `@content` message to a copied `site/config/file_error_message.php` (see [Upload](../library/upload/configuration.mdx)).
- **`htdocs/.htaccess`**: remove the `<IfModule mod_php5.c>` block and the Apache 2.2 `Order` / `Deny from all` lines (500 on Apache 2.4), and add the dotfile rules of `core/dist/htaccess.dist`. `fegg-cli doctor` warns about the old lines.
- **Captcha keys and the database DSN** move to `env.php`: the skeleton's `database_config.php` and `contact/contact_config.php` read them with `feggEnv()`. Copied old files keep working; move the keys out of committed files when convenient.
- **Route-only controllers**: controllers that implement `Fegg\Http\RouteOnly` (such as the skeleton's `Site\Controller\Page`) are only reached through `site/config/route.php`. Add a route such as `$route['about/'] = 'page/render/about';` for each static page that was reached as `/page/render/...` (see [URL](../basic/url.mdx#static-pages)).

### Additional work for v3 projects

- **Mail** (`Fegg\Tool\Mail`) uses PHPMailer. `sendWithFile()`, `setInputCharset()` and `setSendCharset()` were removed: add attachments with `pushAttached()` and call `send()`. A failed send throws `PHPMailer\PHPMailer\Exception`.
- **Trailing slash**: URLs without an extension are redirected (301) to the same URL with a trailing `/`. Check the keys in `route.php` and the links in templates.
- **PHP 8**: fix deprecation warnings in the project code (for example passing `null` to string functions).

## 5. Check

- [ ] `./fegg-cli template:lint` prints OK
- [ ] `php -l` reports no syntax errors for the PHP files under `htdocs/_fegg` (excluding `vendor/` and `data/`)
- [ ] `./fegg-cli migration` against a copy of the production database only adds new core tables such as `cms_login_attempt` (no project table definition is missing)
- [ ] `./fegg-cli core:status` prints `No changes to core files.`
- [ ] Public pages, the contact form (through to sending), CMS login, create / edit / delete for each post type, and media and image uploads work
- [ ] Every post type appears in the CMS side menu and on the dashboard
- [ ] Every saving action in `site/controller/Cms/` calls `$this->requirePost()`: opening its URL in the browser (a GET, e.g. `/cms/news/update/`) answers 405
- [ ] No page links to an action named like a method of `Fegg\Application` (`mail`, `in`, `redirect` ...) or defined only in a base controller (both are 404 now)
- [ ] The PHP error log has no warnings and no `Fegg deprecated` entries
- [ ] After deploying, `./fegg-cli doctor https://<production URL>` prints OK and the CMS dashboard shows no protection warnings

To roll back, redeploy the old branch. The table definitions are compatible, so the same database file works before and after the migration.


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library ---


# About Extension libraries

Fegg's goal is lightweight and less typing, so the controller base class only has the minimum required functions. Additional functions are provided as library classes in the core (`htdocs/_fegg/core/src/`).

## How to use

Every library is a namespaced class that is loaded by the autoloader. Import it with `use` and create it with `new`.

```php title="Controller"
<?php
namespace Site\Controller;

defined('FEGG_CORE_DIR') || exit;

use Fegg\Tool\Mail;
use Fegg\Validation;

class Contact extends Controller
{
    public function post()
    {
        $validation = new Validation();
        $validation->required('mail', $this->in('mail'), array('@required', 'Email'));

        if (!$validation->isError()) {
            $mail = new Mail('Mail subject', 'Mail body');
            $mail->send($this->in('mail'), 'Sender name', 'from@example.com');
        }
    }
}
```

:::note

Fegg 4 code such as `$this->getClass('Tool/Mail')` still works in Fegg 5 through `Fegg\Legacy`, but every use is written to the PHP error log as `Fegg deprecated: ...` and it will be removed in Fegg 6. Replace it with `new`.

| Fegg 4 | Fegg 5 |
| --- | --- |
| `$this->getClass('Validation')` | `new \Fegg\Validation()` |
| `$this->getClass('Tool/Mail', $subject, $body)` | `new \Fegg\Tool\Mail($subject, $body)` |
| `$this->getClass('Tool/Upload')` | `new \Fegg\Tool\Upload()` |
| `$this->getClass('ReCAPTCHA', $secret)` | `new \Fegg\Captcha\ReCAPTCHA($secret)` |
| `$this->getClass('DB_pdo')` | `new \Fegg\Database\Pdo()` |

The same applies to the other `Tool/...` classes (`Tool/Date`, `Tool/File`, `Tool/OEmbed`, `Tool/RSS`, `Tool/Trim`), which became `Fegg\Tool\Date` and so on.

:::

## Extension Libraries

| Library | Class | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [Validation](./validation/index.mdx) | `Fegg\Validation` | Providing some functions for validation |
| [Database](./database/index.mdx) | `Fegg\Database\Pdo` | Providing necessary functions for using database |
| [Model](./model/index.mdx) | `Fegg\Model` | Extended Database Library for LH project |
| [File](./file/index.mdx) | `Fegg\Tool\File` | Providing some functions for managing files/directories |
| [Upload](./upload/index.mdx) | `Fegg\Tool\Upload` | Providing some functions for uploading/validating files |
| [Mail](./mail/index.mdx) | `Fegg\Tool\Mail` | Providing some functions for sending email (PHPMailer wrapper) |
| [RSS](./rss/index.mdx) | `Fegg\Tool\RSS` | Providing some functions for analyzing RSS document |
| [Trim](./trim/index.mdx) | `Fegg\Tool\Trim` | Providing some functions for processing image (especially image crop) |
| [Date](./date/index.mdx) | `Fegg\Tool\Date` | Providing some functions for calculating dates |
| [OEmbed](./oembed/index.mdx) | `Fegg\Tool\OEmbed` | Getting the embed URL of a YouTube video etc. through oEmbed |
| [Captcha](./captcha/index.mdx) | `Fegg\Captcha\ReCAPTCHA`, `Fegg\Captcha\Turnstile` | Verifying Google reCAPTCHA / Cloudflare Turnstile responses |

## Project libraries

Put your own classes in `htdocs/_fegg/site/src/` with the `Site\` namespace. They are autoloaded in the same way.

```php title="htdocs/_fegg/site/src/MyLib/Foo.php"
<?php
namespace Site\MyLib;

defined('FEGG_CORE_DIR') || exit;

class Foo
{
}
```

```php title="Controller"
use Site\MyLib\Foo;

$foo = new Foo();
```

## External Libraries

These libraries are bundled in `htdocs/_fegg/core/vendor/` and autoloaded.

- [PHPMailer](https://github.com/PHPMailer/PHPMailer) (7.0)
  - Used by [Mail](./mail/index.mdx). Namespace `PHPMailer\PHPMailer`
  - A failed send throws `PHPMailer\PHPMailer\Exception`
- [Spyc](https://github.com/mustangostang/spyc)
  - YAML document loader (global class `Spyc`)
  - Used by the database migration (`fegg-cli migration`)

JSON documents are handled with PHP's own `json_encode` and `json_decode`.


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/captcha ---


import { CategoryDocCardList } from '@site/src/components/CategoryDocCardList';

# Introduction

> Verifying Google reCAPTCHA / Cloudflare Turnstile responses

Two classes verify the token posted by a CAPTCHA widget with the service's API:

- `Fegg\Captcha\ReCAPTCHA`: [Google reCAPTCHA v3](https://developers.google.com/recaptcha/docs/v3) (score based)
- `Fegg\Captcha\Turnstile`: [Cloudflare Turnstile](https://developers.cloudflare.com/turnstile/)

Both send the token with `Fegg\Http\Client` (cURL when it is installed, otherwise PHP's http stream wrapper; 5 second timeout, 3 seconds to connect).

**When the service cannot be reached** (timeout, outage, an answer that is not HTTP 200 JSON), `auth()` fails: `success` is `false` and `errors` has a message asking the visitor to try again later (`確認サービスに接続できませんでした。…`). A warning is written to the [application log](../../basic/logging.mdx). A form protected by a captcha is never accepted unchecked.

## How to use

Import the class and create it with `new`, passing the secret key. Then pass the posted token to `auth()`.

```php title="Controller"
<?php
namespace Site\Controller;

defined('FEGG_CORE_DIR') || exit;

use Fegg\Captcha\ReCAPTCHA;
use Fegg\Captcha\Turnstile;
use Fegg\Validation;

class Entry extends Controller
{
    public function confirm()
    {
        $validation = new Validation();

        // reCAPTCHA (the secret comes from site/config/env.php)
        $recaptcha = new ReCAPTCHA(feggEnv('FEGG_RECAPTCHA_SECRET', ''), 0.5);
        $result = $recaptcha->auth($this->in('g-recaptcha-response'));
        if (!$result['success']) {
            $validation->setError('g-recaptcha-response', implode(',', $result['errors']));
        }

        // Cloudflare Turnstile
        $turnstile = new Turnstile(feggEnv('FEGG_TURNSTILE_SECRET', ''));
        $result = $turnstile->auth($this->in('cf-turnstile-response'));
        if (!$result['success']) {
            $validation->setError('cf-turnstile-response', implode(',', $result['errors']));
        }
    }
}
```

## Keys

The keys differ per server, and the secret keys must not be committed. Put them in `site/config/env.php` (or environment variables) and read them with `feggEnv()`:

```php title="htdocs/_fegg/site/config/env.php"
'FEGG_RECAPTCHA_SITE_KEY' => '...',
'FEGG_RECAPTCHA_SECRET'   => '...',
'FEGG_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY' => '...',
'FEGG_TURNSTILE_SECRET'   => '...',
```

Use different keys (or the providers' test keys) for development and production. On a test server the keys can go in the committed `site/config/env/staging.php` (see [Test servers deployed with git pull](../../first/config.mdx#test-servers-deployed-with-git-pull)):

| | Site key | Secret |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Turnstile, always passes | `1x00000000000000000000AA` | `1x0000000000000000000000000000000AA` |
| Turnstile, always fails | `2x00000000000000000000AB` | `2x0000000000000000000000000000000AA` |

Test secrets accept only the dummy token of the test site keys ([Cloudflare](https://developers.cloudflare.com/turnstile/troubleshooting/testing/)); never use them in production. reCAPTCHA v3 has no test keys: register the test server's domain, and set `FEGG_RECAPTCHA_SCORE_THRESHOLD` to `1.0` to see a rejection. When a captcha is enabled but a key is empty, every form is rejected and an error is logged.

## Contact form

The contact form of the skeleton supports both. Enable one in `htdocs/_fegg/site/config/contact/contact_config.php` with `$_enable_recaptcha` or `$_enable_turnstile`; the file reads the keys from `feggEnv()`:

```php title="htdocs/_fegg/site/config/contact/contact_config.php"
$_enable_recaptcha = true;
$_recaptcha_key    = feggEnv('FEGG_RECAPTCHA_SITE_KEY', '');
$_recaptcha_secret = feggEnv('FEGG_RECAPTCHA_SECRET', '');
$_recaptcha_score_threshold = 0.5;
```

The widgets are output by `site/template/template/antispam.tpl`, and `site/controller/Contact.php` verifies them. Projects whose old `contact_config.php` has the keys written in it keep working; move them to `env.php` when convenient.

## Methods

<CategoryDocCardList label="Methods" />


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/captcha/methods/recaptcha-construct ---


# ReCAPTCHA::\_\_construct

> Set the reCAPTCHA secret key and score threshold

## Description

```php
public ReCAPTCHA::__construct( string $secret, [ float $threshold = 0.5 ] )
```

## Arguments

| Name       | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                 |
| ---------- | --------- | ------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| $secret    | string    |         | reCAPTCHA secret key. Read it from `site/config/env.php` with `feggEnv('FEGG_RECAPTCHA_SECRET')` |
| $threshold | float     | 0.5     | Minimum score (0.0 to 1.0) / a lower score fails [ReCAPTCHA::auth](./recaptcha-auth.mdx) |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Captcha\ReCAPTCHA;

$recaptcha = new ReCAPTCHA(feggEnv('FEGG_RECAPTCHA_SECRET', ''), 0.5);
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/captcha/methods/recaptcha-auth ---


# ReCAPTCHA::auth

> Verify a reCAPTCHA token

## Description

```php
public array ReCAPTCHA::auth( string $response, [ float $threshold = null ] )
```

Sends the token to `https://www.google.com/recaptcha/api/siteverify` and returns an array:

| Key     | Data type | Remarks                                                                  |
| ------- | --------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| success | boolean   | `true` when the API returned success, the score is not below the threshold and there is no error |
| errors  | array     | Error messages (English for the API error codes; a Japanese message when the score is below the threshold) |

When the service cannot be reached or does not answer with HTTP 200 JSON, `success` is `false` and `errors` has one message asking the visitor to try again later. A warning is written to the application log.

## Arguments

| Name      | Data type | Default | Remarks                                              |
| --------- | --------- | ------- | ---------------------------------------------------- |
| $response | string    |         | Token posted by the widget (`g-recaptcha-response`)   |
| $threshold | float    | null    | Minimum score for this call. `null`: the threshold given to the constructor |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Captcha\ReCAPTCHA;

$recaptcha = new ReCAPTCHA(feggEnv('FEGG_RECAPTCHA_SECRET', ''), 0.5);
$result = $recaptcha->auth($this->in('g-recaptcha-response'));

if (!$result['success']) {
    $errorMessage = implode(',', $result['errors']);
}
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/captcha/methods/turnstile-construct ---


# Turnstile::\_\_construct

> Set the Turnstile secret key

## Description

```php
public Turnstile::__construct( string $secret )
```

## Arguments

| Name    | Data type | Default | Remarks              |
| ------- | --------- | ------- | -------------------- |
| $secret | string    |         | Turnstile secret key. Read it from `site/config/env.php` with `feggEnv('FEGG_TURNSTILE_SECRET')` |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Captcha\Turnstile;

$turnstile = new Turnstile(feggEnv('FEGG_TURNSTILE_SECRET', ''));
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/captcha/methods/turnstile-auth ---


# Turnstile::auth

> Verify a Turnstile token

## Description

```php
public array Turnstile::auth( string $response )
```

Sends the token to `https://challenges.cloudflare.com/turnstile/v0/siteverify` and returns an array:

| Key     | Data type | Remarks                                    |
| ------- | --------- | ------------------------------------------ |
| success | boolean   | `true` when the API returned success       |
| errors  | array     | Error messages for the API error codes     |

When the service cannot be reached or does not answer with HTTP 200 JSON, `success` is `false` and `errors` has one message asking the visitor to try again later. A warning is written to the application log.

## Arguments

| Name      | Data type | Default | Remarks                                              |
| --------- | --------- | ------- | ---------------------------------------------------- |
| $response | string    |         | Token posted by the widget (`cf-turnstile-response`)  |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Captcha\Turnstile;

$turnstile = new Turnstile(feggEnv('FEGG_TURNSTILE_SECRET', ''));
$result = $turnstile->auth($this->in('cf-turnstile-response'));

if (!$result['success']) {
    $errorMessage = implode(',', $result['errors']);
}
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/database ---


import { CategoryDocCardList } from '@site/src/components/CategoryDocCardList';

# Introduction

> Run SQL on the database connection

`Fegg\Database\Pdo` builds and runs SQL on top of PHP's PDO. [Fegg\Model](../model/index.mdx) uses it for every query.

:::note

Usually you don't need this class: use [Fegg\Model](../model/index.mdx), which also checks names, applies the visible flag and pages results. Use `Pdo` for raw SQL the model cannot express.

The Fegg 4 class name `DB_pdo` (and `$this->getClass('DB_pdo')`) still works through a compatibility layer, but every use is reported as `Fegg deprecated: ...` in the PHP error log, and it will be removed in Fegg 6.

:::

## How to use

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Database\Pdo;

$db = new Pdo();

// Raw SQL with ? placeholders
$rows = $db->setQuery(
    'SELECT category, count(*) AS n FROM cms_news WHERE valid = 1 GROUP BY category HAVING n > ?',
    array(1)
)->execute()->all();

// Or build the query
$rows = $db->setWhere('category = ?', 2)->setOrder('post_date DESC')->select('cms_news')->execute()->all();
```

A query is built with the `set*` methods and a build method ([select](./methods/select.mdx), [count](./methods/count.mdx), [insert](./methods/insert.mdx), [update](./methods/update.mdx), [delete](./methods/delete.mdx) ...), or written with [setQuery](./methods/setquery.mdx). [execute](./methods/execute.mdx) runs it, then [all](./methods/all.mdx), [one](./methods/one.mdx), [getAffectedRow](./methods/getaffectedrow.mdx) or [getLastIndexId](./methods/getlastindexid.mdx) read the result. After `execute()` the built query is forgotten, so each query starts clean.

### Automatic queries

Queries built with the build methods get the automatic parts of `database_regular_use_query.php` (see [Configuration](./configuration.mdx)): `valid = 1` on SELECT and count, `created` / `modified` / `valid` on INSERT and `modified` on UPDATE. Queries written with `setQuery()` get nothing added.

## Values and placeholders

- Values are always bound to `?` placeholders. Integers and booleans are bound as integers, `null` as NULL, everything else as strings (so `count(*) > ?` works on SQLite).
- In [setWhere](./methods/setwhere.mdx), [setHaving](./methods/sethaving.mdx) and [setOn](./methods/seton.mdx), an array bound to one placeholder expands it: `id = ?` and `id IN (?)` become `id IN (?, ?, ...)`, `<>` / `!=` / `NOT IN` become `NOT IN`, `LIKE ?` becomes `(... LIKE ? OR ... LIKE ?)` and `NOT LIKE ?` becomes `(... NOT LIKE ? AND ...)`. An empty array matches no row (`=`, `IN`, `LIKE`) or every row (`<>`, `NOT IN`, `NOT LIKE`). An array on any other operator throws `InvalidArgumentException`.
- A `?` inside a quoted string literal (`'?'`) is not a placeholder.
- Table, column and ORDER BY text given to the `set*` methods is written into the SQL as it is: never put user input in it.

## Errors

A failed connection or query throws `Fegg\Database\QueryException` (a `RuntimeException`). Its message ends with the SQL with its values filled in, for example `no such column: nope (SQL: SELECT nope FROM cms_news WHERE title = 'it''s')`. `getSql()` returns the SQL with its `?` placeholders and `getParameters()` the values. Uncaught, it becomes an HTTP 500 and is written to the PHP error log and the [application log](../../basic/logging.mdx). Fegg 4 printed the error (to developer IPs only) and called `exit` instead.

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Database\QueryException;

try {
    $db->setQuery($sql, $params)->execute();
} catch (QueryException $e) {
    // e.g. a duplicate key
    error_log($e->getMessage());
}
```

## Connections and transactions

- Connections are opened once per DSN and user name and shared by every `Pdo` instance and every model.
- SELECT and PRAGMA queries run on a slave, other queries on the master (see [Configuration](./configuration.mdx)). [masterServer](./methods/masterserver.mdx) and [slaveServer](./methods/slaveserver.mdx) choose the server for the next query.
- [beginTransaction](./methods/begintransaction.mdx) opens a transaction on the master. Until [commit](./methods/commit.mdx) or [rollBack](./methods/rollback.mdx), every query of every instance and every model runs inside it. [Model::transaction](../model/methods/transaction.mdx) does this for you.

## Methods

# Configuration

### Database connect setting

The connection is set in **htdocs/\_fegg/site/config/database_config.php**. The skeleton's file reads the values from `feggEnv()` (an environment variable, then `site/config/env.php`, see [Settings](../../first/config.mdx#environments-and-secrets)), so the file itself is committed and the same on every server. Without any value it uses the SQLite file `site/data/db/db.db`.

```php title="site/config/database_config.php"
<?php
defined('FEGG_CORE_DIR') || exit;

/**
 * Database connection settings
 *
 *   FEGG_DB_DSN       PDO DSN. Default: the SQLite file below
 *   FEGG_DB_USER      user name (MySQL)
 *   FEGG_DB_PASSWORD  password (MySQL)
 *   FEGG_DB_SLAVE_DSN read-only replica (optional; default: the master)
 *   FEGG_DB_PATH      SQLite file path. As an environment variable it wins over FEGG_DB_DSN
 *                     in env.php (the test suite uses it)
 */

$_sqlite_only = getenv('FEGG_DB_PATH') !== false && getenv('FEGG_DB_DSN') === false;
if ($_sqlite_only) {
    $_dsn = 'sqlite:'.getenv('FEGG_DB_PATH');
} else {
    $_dsn = feggEnv('FEGG_DB_DSN', 'sqlite:'.feggEnv('FEGG_DB_PATH', FEGG_SITE_DIR.'/data/db/db.db'));
}

// Master database (a single server)
$database_config['master'] = array(
    'dsn'      => $_dsn,
    'username' => feggEnv('FEGG_DB_USER', ''),
    'password' => feggEnv('FEGG_DB_PASSWORD', ''),
);

// Slave databases (multiple servers allowed)
$database_config['slave'] = array(
    array(
        'dsn'      => $_sqlite_only ? $_dsn : feggEnv('FEGG_DB_SLAVE_DSN', $_dsn),
        'username' => $database_config['master']['username'],
        'password' => $database_config['master']['password'],
    ),
);
```

- `master` is used for INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE, schema changes and transactions. SELECT and PRAGMA queries use one of the `slave` entries, picked at random. With a single database, both use the same DSN.
- `dsn`, `username` and `password` are passed to PHP's `PDO`.
- A connection that fails throws `Fegg\Database\QueryException` instead of printing the error and exiting.
- `new \Fegg\Database\Pdo('name')` reads `name/database_config.php` and `name/database_regular_use_query.php` instead, for a second database.
- Projects created before this file used `feggEnv()` may keep their own `database_config.php`. Move the passwords out of committed files into `env.php` when convenient.

### MySQL

SQLite is the default. For MySQL (5.7 / 8.x) or MariaDB, set the connection in `site/config/env.php` (or as environment variables):

```php title="site/config/env.php"
'FEGG_DB_DSN'      => 'mysql:host=127.0.0.1;port=3306;dbname=example;charset=utf8mb4',
'FEGG_DB_USER'     => 'example',
'FEGG_DB_PASSWORD' => '...',
```

- Always add **`charset=utf8mb4`** to the DSN, or emoji and other 4-byte characters are lost.
- The connection runs with `sql_mode = ANSI_QUOTES`: Fegg quotes names with `"`, so in raw SQL write string literals with **single quotes** (`WHERE title = 'foo'`, not `"foo"`, which is a column name). Queries are buffered.
- `./fegg-cli migration`, `migration:db2schema` and `/cms/migrate/` work on MySQL as on SQLite. Tables are created as InnoDB with `utf8mb4_unicode_ci`, and changed tables are altered in place (`ADD` / `MODIFY` / `DROP COLUMN`).

The YAML types are mapped like this (a size written in the YAML, such as `VARCHAR(100)`, is used as written):

| YAML type  | MySQL type     |
| :--------- | :------------- |
| `INTEGER`  | `INT`          |
| `CHAR`     | `VARCHAR(255)` |
| `TEXT`     | `MEDIUMTEXT`   |
| `BOOLEAN`  | `TINYINT(1)`   |
| `DATETIME` | `DATETIME`     |
| `BLOB`     | `LONGBLOB`     |
| `REAL`     | `DOUBLE`       |

The YAML type is kept in the column comment (`fegg:CHAR`), so `migration:db2schema` writes the same YAML back. Indexes on `TEXT` columns use the first 191 characters.

:::danger

As on SQLite, `fegg-cli migration` **drops every table that is not in a YAML file**, and removes columns that are not in the YAML. Give the site a database of its own (never one shared with another application), and check the pending changes on `/cms/migrate/` when in doubt: it lists them before the "Run migration" button applies them.

:::

The skeleton's MySQL tests run with `composer test:mysql` against the server set in `FEGG_TEST_MYSQL_DSN`, `FEGG_TEST_MYSQL_USER` and `FEGG_TEST_MYSQL_PASSWORD`. Each test creates and drops its own database.

### Automatic append query configuration

The queries added automatically to queries built by `Fegg\Model` and the build methods of `Fegg\Database\Pdo` are set in **htdocs/\_fegg/core/config/database_regular_use_query.php**. Do not edit the core: to change them, copy the file to `htdocs/_fegg/site/config/database_regular_use_query.php` (config files are looked up in `site/config` first, then `core/config`).

`CURRENT_TABLE` is replaced with the table name, and `now()` with the current date and time. An item that the query already sets (for example `valid` given to `insertRow()`) is not added again.

```php title="core/config/database_regular_use_query.php"
<?php
defined('FEGG_CORE_DIR') || exit;

/**
 * Automatically appended queries
 *
 * Queries appended automatically when the "select", "insert", "update" and "delete" methods of the DB class run
 * (to be extended as needed).
 */
$database_regular_use_query = array(
    // Queries appended regardless of the table
    'regular_use' => array(
        'select' => array(
            'where' => 'CURRENT_TABLE.valid = 1',
            'order' => '',
        ),
        'count' => array(
            'where' => 'CURRENT_TABLE.valid = 1',
            'order' => '',
        ),
        'insert' => array(
            'item' => 'modified = now(), created = now(), valid = 1',
        ),
        'update' => array(
            'item' => "modified = now()",
            'where' => '',
        ),
    ),

    /*
    // Queries appended per table
    'table' => array(
        'table_name' => array(
            'select' => array(
                'where' => 'show_flag = 0',
                'order' => '',
            ),
            'insert' => array(
                'item' => 'update_time = now(), create_datetime = now()',
            ),
            'update' => array(
                'item' => 'update_time = now()',
                'where' => '',
            ),
        ),
    ),
    */
);
```

This is why every table used with `Fegg\Model` needs `valid`, `created` and `modified` columns. To skip these queries, see `unsetRegularUseQuery()`, `unsetRegularUseQueryForTable()` and `disabledRegularUseQuery()` ([Model](../model/methods/unsetregularusequery.mdx), [Pdo](./methods/unsetregularusequery.mdx)).


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/database/methods/construct ---


# Pdo::\_\_construct

> Pdo class constructor

## Description

```php
public Pdo::__construct( [ string $env = '' ] )
```

Loads `database_config.php` and `database_regular_use_query.php` (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)). Nothing is connected until the first query; connections are shared by every instance.

## Arguments

| Name | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                                              |
| ---- | --------- | ------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| $env | string    | ''      | Config sub directory: `'name'` reads `name/database_config.php` and `name/database_regular_use_query.php` |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Database\Pdo;

$db = new Pdo();
$rows = $db->setQuery('SELECT * FROM cms_news WHERE valid = 1 AND category = ?', array(2))->execute()->all();
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/database/methods/select ---


# Pdo::select

> Set select query

## Description

```php
public self Pdo::select( string $table )
```

Builds a SELECT on `$table` from what was set with [setItem](./setitem.mdx) (columns, default `*`), [setJoin](./setjoin.mdx) / [setOn](./seton.mdx), [setWhere](./setwhere.mdx), [setGroup](./setgroup.mdx), [setHaving](./sethaving.mdx), [setOrder](./setorder.mdx) and [setLimit](./setlimit.mdx). `CURRENT_TABLE.valid = 1` is added (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)). Run it with [execute](./execute.mdx).

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks           |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ----------------- |
| $table | string    |         | Target table name |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
$db = new \Fegg\Database\Pdo();

// Select * From "cms_news" Where category = ? And "cms_news".valid = 1 Order By post_date DESC Limit 0, 10
$rows = $db->setWhere('category = ?', 2)
    ->setOrder('post_date DESC')
    ->setLimit(10)
    ->select('cms_news')
    ->execute()
    ->all();
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/database/methods/count ---


# Pdo::count

> Set select count query

## Description

```php
public self Pdo::count( string $table )
```

Builds `Select Count(*) as number_of_records From (...) AS "count_temp"` around the SELECT that [select](./select.mdx) would build, so conditions, joins and groups are counted correctly. Read the result with `one()['number_of_records']`.

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks           |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ----------------- |
| $table | string    |         | Target table name |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
$db = new \Fegg\Database\Pdo();

$record = $db->setWhere('category = ?', 2)->count('cms_news')->execute()->one();
$count = (int) $record['number_of_records'];
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/database/methods/insert ---


# Pdo::insert

> Set insert query

## Description

```php
public self Pdo::insert( string $table )
```

Builds an INSERT on `$table` from the values set with [setValues](./setvalues.mdx) (or [setItem](./setitem.mdx)). `created`, `modified` and `valid` are added unless given (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)). Run it with [execute](./execute.mdx) and read the new ID with [getLastIndexId](./getlastindexid.mdx).

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks           |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ----------------- |
| $table | string    |         | Target table name |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
$db = new \Fegg\Database\Pdo();

// Insert Into "cms_news" ("title", "category", modified, created, valid) Values (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
$db->setValues(array('title' => 'Sample', 'category' => 2))->insert('cms_news')->execute();
$id = $db->getLastIndexId();
// -> '24'
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/database/methods/update ---


# Pdo::update

> Set update query

## Description

```php
public self Pdo::update( string $table )
```

Builds an UPDATE on `$table` from the values set with [setValues](./setvalues.mdx) (or [setItem](./setitem.mdx)) and [setIncrement](./setincrement.mdx), and the condition set with [setWhere](./setwhere.mdx). `modified` is added unless given. Run it with [execute](./execute.mdx) and read the number of updated rows with [getAffectedRow](./getaffectedrow.mdx).

Unlike `Model::updateRowsWhere()`, `Pdo` does not refuse an UPDATE without a condition: without `setWhere()` every row is updated.

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks           |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ----------------- |
| $table | string    |         | Target table name |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
$db = new \Fegg\Database\Pdo();

// Update "cms_news" Set "title"= ?, modified = ? Where news_id IN (?, ?)
$db->setValues(array('title' => 'New title'))
    ->setWhere('news_id IN (?)', array(1, 2))
    ->update('cms_news')
    ->execute();
$updated = $db->getAffectedRow();
// -> 2
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/database/methods/delete ---


# Pdo::delete

> Set delete query

## Description

```php
public self Pdo::delete( [ string $table = '' ] )
```

Builds a `DELETE` on `$table` with the condition set with [setWhere](./setwhere.mdx). This removes the rows; for a soft delete (`valid = 0`) use `Model::delete()`.

`Pdo` does not refuse a DELETE without a condition: without `setWhere()` every row is removed.

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks           |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ----------------- |
| $table | string    | ''      | Target table name |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
$db = new \Fegg\Database\Pdo();

// Delete From "cms_news" Where news_id = ?
$db->setWhere('news_id = ?', 3)->delete('cms_news')->execute();
$deleted = $db->getAffectedRow();
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/database/methods/truncate ---


# Pdo::truncate

> Set truncate query

## Description

```php
public self Pdo::truncate( string $table )
```

Builds `Truncate "table"`, which removes every row. MySQL only: SQLite has no TRUNCATE (use [delete](./delete.mdx) without a condition there).

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks           |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ----------------- |
| $table | string    |         | Target table name |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
$db = new \Fegg\Database\Pdo();

// Truncate "sample_log"
$db->truncate('sample_log')->execute();
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/database/methods/createtable ---


# Pdo::createTable

> Set create table query

## Description

```php
public self Pdo::createTable( string $table, array $field )
```

Builds a `Create Table`. Tables are normally defined in `site/config/database/*.yaml` and created with `./fegg-cli migration`.

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks                             |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ----------------------------------- |
| $table | string    |         | Table name                          |
| $field | array     |         | List of column definitions (below)  |

### Column definition

Every key must be present.

| Key          | Data type | Remarks                                          |
| ------------ | --------- | ------------------------------------------------ |
| `name`       | string    | Column name                                      |
| `type`       | string    | Column type                                      |
| `notnull`    | boolean   | Add NOT NULL                                     |
| `dflt_value` | mixed     | Default value (`null`, `''` or `0`: no default)  |
| `pk`         | boolean   | Add PRIMARY KEY                                  |
| `ai`         | boolean   | Add AUTOINCREMENT                                |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
$db = new \Fegg\Database\Pdo();

// Create Table "sample" (id INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, title TEXT DEFAULT 'none')
$db->createTable('sample', array(
    array('name' => 'id',    'type' => 'INTEGER', 'notnull' => true,  'dflt_value' => null,   'pk' => true,  'ai' => true),
    array('name' => 'title', 'type' => 'TEXT',    'notnull' => false, 'dflt_value' => 'none', 'pk' => false, 'ai' => false),
))->execute();
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/database/methods/createindex ---


# Pdo::createIndex

> Set create index query

## Description

```php
public self Pdo::createIndex( string $table, array $field )
```

Builds `Create [UNIQUE] Index "name" ON "table" (columns)`.

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks                    |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | -------------------------- |
| $table | string    |         | Table name                 |
| $field | array     |         | Index definition (below)   |

### Index definition

| Key      | Data type | Remarks                              |
| -------- | --------- | ------------------------------------ |
| `name`   | string    | Index name                           |
| `unique` | boolean   | Create a UNIQUE index                |
| `target` | string    | Columns, separated by commas         |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
$db = new \Fegg\Database\Pdo();

// Create UNIQUE Index "sample_title" ON "sample" (title)
$db->createIndex('sample', array('name' => 'sample_title', 'unique' => true, 'target' => 'title'))->execute();
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/database/methods/alteraddcolumn ---


# Pdo::alterAddColumn

> Set create alter add column query

## Description

```php
public self Pdo::alterAddColumn( string $table, array $field )
```

Builds `Alter Table "table" ADD COLUMN ...`.

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                  |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| $table | string    |         | Table name                                                               |
| $field | array     |         | Column definition, with every key described in [createTable](./createtable.mdx) |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
$db = new \Fegg\Database\Pdo();

// Alter Table "sample" ADD COLUMN note TEXT
$db->alterAddColumn('sample', array(
    'name'       => 'note',
    'type'       => 'TEXT',
    'notnull'    => false,
    'dflt_value' => null,
    'pk'         => false,
    'ai'         => false,
))->execute();
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/database/methods/alterrenameto ---


# Pdo::alterRenameTo

> Set create alter rename to query

## Description

```php
public self Pdo::alterRenameTo( string $table, string $rename )
```

Builds `Alter Table "table" RENAME TO "rename"`.

## Arguments

| Name    | Data type | Default | Remarks         |
| ------- | --------- | ------- | --------------- |
| $table  | string    |         | Table name      |
| $rename | string    |         | New table name  |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
$db = new \Fegg\Database\Pdo();

// Alter Table "sample" RENAME TO "sample_old"
$db->alterRenameTo('sample', 'sample_old')->execute();
```
