import { CategoryDocCardList } from '@site/src/components/CategoryDocCardList';

# Introduction

> Providing some functions for processing image (especially image crop)

`Fegg\Tool\Trim` resizes and crops JPEG, GIF and PNG images with the GD extension. JPEG images are rotated according to their Exif orientation.

## How to use

Import `Fegg\Tool\Trim` and create it with `new`. Set the source image and the output path with [Trim::setImage](./methods/setimage.mdx), resize it with one of the `resize*` methods, then save it with [Trim::create](./methods/create.mdx).

```php title="Controller"
<?php
namespace Site\Controller;

defined('FEGG_CORE_DIR') || exit;

use Fegg\Tool\Trim;

class Thumbnail extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        $trim = new Trim();
        $trim->setImage('path/to/image.jpg', 'path/to/thumbnail/300_300.jpg');

        $trim->resizeTrim(300, 300, 'm');
        $trim->create();

        // Output the saved image to the browser
        $trim->view();
    }
}
```

The thumbnails of CMS images (`Fegg\Controller\Up_dir::image()`) are generated with this class.

## Methods

<CategoryDocCardList label="Methods" />


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/trim/methods/setimage ---


# Trim::setImage

> Set target image and output file path

## Description

```php
public void Trim::setImage( string $org_path, string $cnv_path )
```

Loads the source image. The output format is decided by the extension of `$cnv_path`.

:::caution

This method stops the script with `exit` when the source file does not exist (`Not File`), when the output extension is not `jpg`, `gif` or `png` (`Error Extension`; `.jpeg` is not accepted), or when the source is not a JPEG, GIF or PNG image (`NotImage`). Check the files beforehand.

:::

## Arguments

| Name      | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                 |
| --------- | --------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------- |
| $org_path | string    |         | Source image path                                       |
| $cnv_path | string    |         | Output image path (`.jpg`, `.gif` or `.png`)            |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Trim;

$trim = new Trim();
$trim->setImage('path/to/image.jpg', 'path/to/converted.jpg');
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/trim/methods/resize ---


# Trim::resize

> Resize the image to fit in the specified size with keeping ratio

## Description

```php
public void Trim::resize( int $w, int $h )
```

The image is scaled down to fit in `$w` x `$h`, keeping its aspect ratio. The output may be smaller than `$w` x `$h` in one direction. Images smaller than the specified size are not enlarged. `0` means the original width or height.

## Arguments

| Name | Data type | Default | Remarks                                        |
| ---- | --------- | ------- | ---------------------------------------------- |
| $w   | int       |         | Maximum width / `0`: original width            |
| $h   | int       |         | Maximum height / `0`: original height          |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Trim;

$trim = new Trim();
$trim->setImage('path/to/image.jpg', 'path/to/converted.jpg');

// Width 300px at most, any height
$trim->resize(300, 0);
$trim->create();
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/trim/methods/resizefill ---


# Trim::resizeFill

> Resize the image with keeping ratio and filling the empty area (Like `object-fit: contain`)

## Description

```php
public void Trim::resizeFill( int $w, int $h )
```

The output is exactly `$w` x `$h`. The image is scaled down to fit in it, keeping its aspect ratio, and placed in the center. The empty area is white for JPEG output and transparent for PNG output from a PNG source. Images smaller than the specified size are not enlarged. `0` means the original width or height.

## Arguments

| Name | Data type | Default | Remarks                                 |
| ---- | --------- | ------- | --------------------------------------- |
| $w   | int       |         | Output width / `0`: original width      |
| $h   | int       |         | Output height / `0`: original height    |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Trim;

$trim = new Trim();
$trim->setImage('path/to/image.png', 'path/to/converted.png');

$trim->resizeFill(300, 300);
$trim->create();
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/trim/methods/resizeforce ---


# Trim::resizeForce

> Resize the image to the specified size ignoring ratio

## Description

```php
public void Trim::resizeForce( int $w, int $h )
```

The image is stretched to exactly `$w` x `$h` (Like `object-fit: fill`). `0` means the original width or height.

## Arguments

| Name | Data type | Default | Remarks                                 |
| ---- | --------- | ------- | --------------------------------------- |
| $w   | int       |         | Output width / `0`: original width      |
| $h   | int       |         | Output height / `0`: original height    |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Trim;

$trim = new Trim();
$trim->setImage('path/to/image.jpg', 'path/to/converted.jpg');

$trim->resizeForce(300, 300);
$trim->create();
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/trim/methods/resizetrim ---


# Trim::resizeTrim

> Resize the image with keeping ratio and trimming the overflow (Like `object-fit: cover`)

## Description

```php
public void Trim::resizeTrim( int $w, int $h, string $cp )
```

The output is exactly `$w` x `$h`. The image is scaled to cover the whole area, keeping its aspect ratio, and the overflow is cut off. `$cp` decides which part of the image is kept. `0` means the original width or height.

## Arguments

| Name | Data type | Default | Remarks                                 |
| ---- | --------- | ------- | --------------------------------------- |
| $w   | int       |         | Output width / `0`: original width      |
| $h   | int       |         | Output height / `0`: original height    |
| $cp  | string    |         | Crop position (see below)               |

### Crop position

| $cp  | Kept part                       |
| ---- | ------------------------------- |
| `t`  | Top center                      |
| `rt` | Top right                       |
| `l`  | Middle left                     |
| `m`  | Center                          |
| `r`  | Middle right                    |
| `b`  | Bottom center                   |
| `lb` | Middle left (same as `l`)       |
| `rb` | Bottom center (same as `b`)     |
| `lt` or any other value | Top left |

:::note

In the current implementation `lb` and `rb` do not crop at the bottom corners: they behave like `l` and `b`.

:::

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Trim;

$trim = new Trim();
$trim->setImage('path/to/image.jpg', 'path/to/converted.jpg');

$trim->resizeTrim(300, 300, 'm');
$trim->create();
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/trim/methods/create ---

> Save the image to the output path

## Description

```php
public void Trim::create()
```

Saves the resized image to the output path given to [Trim::setImage](./setimage.mdx). Missing directories are created. JPEG images are saved with quality 90.

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Trim;

$trim = new Trim();
$trim->setImage('path/to/image.jpg', 'path/to/converted.jpg');

$trim->resizeTrim(300, 300, 'm');
$trim->create();
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/trim/methods/view ---

> Output the saved image to the browser

## Description

```php
public void Trim::view()
```

Sends the `Content-type` header and outputs the file at the output path. Call [Trim::create](./create.mdx) first: nothing is output when the file does not exist.

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Trim;

$trim = new Trim();
$trim->setImage('path/to/image.jpg', 'path/to/converted.jpg');

$trim->resizeTrim(300, 300, 'm');
$trim->create();
$trim->view();
```

import { CategoryDocCardList } from '@site/src/components/CategoryDocCardList';

# Introduction

> Providing some functions for uploading/validating files

## How to use

Import `Fegg\Tool\Upload` and create it with `new`. Set the upload directory, then call [Upload::upload](./methods/upload.mdx) with the `name` of the `input[type="file"]`.

```php title="Controller"
<?php
namespace Site\Controller;

defined('FEGG_CORE_DIR') || exit;

use Fegg\Tool\Upload;

class Entry extends Controller
{
    public function post()
    {
        $this->loadConfig('upload_dir');

        $upload = new Upload();
        $upload->setUploadDir($this->config['upload_dir']['path'] . '/entry');
        $upload->setMaxFileSize(5);
        $upload->setFileType(array('jpg', 'jpeg', 'png', 'pdf'));

        if ($upload->upload('file', uniqid('f_'), 'Attachment')) {
            $data = $upload->getUploadedData('file');
            // $data['name'], $data['org_name'], $data['type'], $data['size'] ...
        } else {
            $errors = $upload->getErrorMessage('file');
        }
    }
}
```

`$this->loadConfig('upload_dir')` reads `site/config/upload_dir.php` (`path` and `url` of `htdocs/up_dir`) into `$this->config['upload_dir']`. The CMS screens (`Fegg\Controller\Cms\Image`, `Media`, `Multi`) use this class in the same way.

Error messages are defined in `file_error_message.php`, see [Configuration](./configuration.mdx).

## What is checked

- The extension must be allowed by [setFileType](./methods/setfiletype.mdx). Script extensions (`php`, `phtml`, `phar`, `cgi` ...) are refused even when listed.
- The content must match the extension: `getimagesize()` for images, the `finfo` MIME type for known types. A mismatch is the error `@content`.
- Files are saved under the name you give (`uniqid('f_')`, ...), never under the visitor's file name. Keep the original name (`org_name`) in the database only.
- Directories are created with mode `0755` and files saved with `0644`.

## Protecting the upload directory

When the upload directory is below the document root, [setUploadDir](./methods/setuploaddir.mdx) writes an `.htaccess` into it that denies and disables scripts (`.php`, `.phtml`, `.phar`, `.cgi` ...), so an uploaded file can never run as code. The CMS dashboard also writes `htdocs/up_dir/.htaccess` when it opens, and the skeleton ships the file.

`fegg-cli doctor` and the CMS dashboard report a missing `htdocs/up_dir/.htaccess` (see [fegg-cli](../../first/cli.mdx#doctor)). Remember it when you upload over FTP: many FTP clients skip dotfiles. On nginx, `.htaccess` is ignored: deny scripts under the upload URL yourself.

```nginx
location ~* ^/up_dir/.*\.(php\d?|phtml|phar|pht|cgi|pl|py|sh)$ { deny all; }
```

## Sending stored files

The CMS sends stored files (`/up_dir/file/<id>/`, the media and image screens) with `Fegg\Http\Download::sendHeaders()`. It sends `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`, a valid `Cache-Control`, and an RFC 6266 `Content-Disposition` with the original name escaped (an ASCII fallback plus the UTF-8 name). HTML, SVG, XML and JavaScript files are always sent as attachments, never shown inline. Use it when your own controller sends a stored file:

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Http\Download;

Download::sendHeaders(array(
    'type'     => $file['type'],
    'size'     => filesize($path),
    'org_name' => $file['org_name'],
), false);              // false: browser cache only (private); true: public
$this->halt(file_get_contents($path));
```

## Methods

<CategoryDocCardList label="Methods" />


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/upload/configuration ---


# Configuration

The error messages of [Upload](./index.mdx) are defined in `htdocs/_fegg/core/config/file_error_message.php`.

To change them, do not edit the core: copy the file to `htdocs/_fegg/site/config/file_error_message.php` and edit the copy. The file in `site/config/` is used instead of the core's one (the whole file is replaced, so keep every key).

`@content` was added in Fegg 5. A copy made from an older core file works without it (a built-in Japanese text is used), but add the key to translate or change it.

```php title="htdocs/_fegg/core/config/file_error_message.php"
<?php
defined('FEGG_CORE_DIR') || exit;

/**
 * Error messages for the Tool/File class.
 * @see Tool/File.php
 */
// @file is too big.
$file_error_message['@size']   = '<strong>%s</strong> のサイズが大きすぎます';
// @file is not selected specified type.
$file_error_message['@type']   = '<strong>%s</strong> が指定の型式で選択されていません';
// @file failed to upload.
$file_error_message['@failed'] = '<strong>%s</strong> のアップロードに失敗しました';
// @file's content does not match its extension.
$file_error_message['@content'] = '<strong>%s</strong> の内容がファイル形式と一致しません';
// @file is not selected.
$file_error_message['@empty']  = '<strong>%s</strong> が選択されていません';
```

`%s` is replaced with the `$label` passed to [Upload::upload](./methods/upload.mdx).

| Code      | When                                                                  |
| --------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `@empty`  | No file was selected (only when the file is required)                 |
| `@size`   | The file is larger than the maximum size, or than the php.ini / form limit |
| `@type`   | The extension is not one of the allowed file types, or is a script extension |
| `@content` | The content does not match the extension (checked with `finfo` and `getimagesize()`) |
| `@failed` | Any other upload error, or the file could not be moved to the upload directory |

## Other languages

When a language code is passed to the constructor (`new Upload('en')`), the messages are read from `config/en/file_error_message.php` (searched in `site/config/` first, then `core/config/`).


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/upload/methods/construct ---


# Upload::\_\_construct

> Create the uploader and load the error messages

```php
public Upload::__construct( [ string $languageCode = null ] )
```

Loads the error messages from `file_error_message.php` (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)). The default settings are: no upload directory, a maximum file size of 2 MB, and the file types `jpg`, `jpeg`, `png` and `gif`.

## Arguments

| Name          | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                          |
| ------------- | --------- | ------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------- |
| $languageCode | string    | null    | Language code / if set, `config/{languageCode}/file_error_message.php` is used |



--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/upload/methods/setuploaddir ---


# Upload::setUploadDir

> Set the upload directory

## Description

```php
public boolean Upload::setUploadDir( string $dir )
```

The directory is created (recursively, mode `0755`) when it does not exist. Returns `true` when the directory is writable, otherwise `false` and the directory is not set.

When the directory is below the document root and has no `.htaccess`, one is written that stops scripts from running there (see [Introduction](../index.mdx#protecting-the-upload-directory)). An existing `.htaccess` is left alone.

## Arguments

| Name | Data type | Default | Remarks          |
| ---- | --------- | ------- | ---------------- |
| $dir | string    |         | Upload directory |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Upload;

$upload = new Upload();
$upload->setUploadDir($this->config['upload_dir']['path']);
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/upload/methods/getuploaddir ---


# Upload::getUploadDir

> Get the upload directory

## Description

```php
public string Upload::getUploadDir()
```

Returns the directory set by [Upload::setUploadDir](./setuploaddir.mdx), or an empty string when none is set.

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Upload;

$upload->setUploadDir('path/to/up_dir');

echo $upload->getUploadDir(); // -> "path/to/up_dir"
```

| ----- | --------- | ------- | ---------------------- |


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/upload/methods/setfiletype ---


# Upload::setFileType

> Set the allowed file types (extensions)

## Description

```php
public boolean Upload::setFileType( array $type )
```

Replaces the list of allowed extensions. The extensions are compared in lower case. Returns `false` when `$type` is not an array. The default is `array('jpg', 'jpeg', 'png', 'gif')`.

Extensions a web server may run as code (`php`, `php5`, `phtml`, `phar`, `pht`, `phps`, `cgi`, `pl`, `py`, `rb`, `sh`, `asp`, `aspx`, `jsp`, `shtml`, `htaccess`, `htpasswd`) are **always refused**, even when listed here. The file's content must also match its extension (see [Upload::upload](./upload.mdx)).

## Arguments

| Name  | Data type | Default | Remarks                                  |
| ----- | --------- | ------- | ---------------------------------------- |
| $type | array     |         | Allowed extensions, without the leading dot |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Upload;

$upload = new Upload();
$upload->setFileType(array('pdf', 'docx', 'xlsx'));
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/upload/methods/getfiletype ---


# Upload::getFileType

> Get the allowed file types (extensions)

## Description

```php
public array Upload::getFileType()
```

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Upload;

$upload = new Upload();
var_dump($upload->getFileType()); // -> array('jpg', 'jpeg', 'png', 'gif')
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/upload/methods/setuploadinfo ---


# Upload::setUploadInfo

> Set the upload options at once

## Description

```php
public boolean Upload::setUploadInfo( array $data )
```

Calls [Upload::setUploadDir](./setuploaddir.mdx), [Upload::setMaxFileSize](./setmaxfilesize.mdx) and [Upload::setFileType](./setfiletype.mdx) for the keys present in `$data`. Returns `false` when one of them fails.

## Arguments

| Name  | Data type | Default | Remarks                    |
| ----- | --------- | ------- | -------------------------- |
| $data | array     |         | Options (see below)        |

| Key         | Data type | Remarks                     |
| ----------- | --------- | --------------------------- |
| upDir       | string    | Upload directory            |
| maxFileSize | numeric   | Maximum file size (MB)      |
| fileType    | array     | Allowed extensions          |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Upload;

$upload = new Upload();
$upload->setUploadInfo(array(
    'upDir'       => $this->config['upload_dir']['path'] . '/entry',
    'maxFileSize' => 5,
    'fileType'    => array('jpg', 'png', 'pdf'),
));
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/upload/methods/upload ---


# Upload::upload

> Validate uploaded file and move to upload directory

## Description

```php
public boolean Upload::upload( string $name, string $up_name, [ string $label = 'ファイル', boolean $required = true ] )
```

Checks the file posted as `$_FILES[$name]` and moves it to `{upload directory}/{$up_name}.{extension}` with mode `0644`. The extension is the lower-cased extension of the original file name.

The checks, in order:

1. Upload errors and the size (`@empty`, `@size`, `@failed`).
2. The extension must be in [setFileType](./setfiletype.mdx), and must not be a script extension (`@type`).
3. The content must match the extension (`@content`): images (`jpg`, `jpeg`, `png`, `gif`, `webp`) must be readable by `getimagesize()`, and for known types (images, `pdf`, Office files, `zip`, `txt`, `csv`) the MIME type reported by `finfo` must fit the extension. A PHP file renamed to `.jpg`, or a text file named `.pdf`, is refused. Other extensions are checked by the extension only.

Returns `true` on success. On failure it returns `false` and the error messages are available with [Upload::getErrorMessage](./geterrormessage.mdx). When `$required` is `false` and no file was selected, it returns `true` without saving anything.

Set the upload directory with [Upload::setUploadDir](./setuploaddir.mdx) before calling this method.

## Arguments

| Name      | Data type | Default    | Remarks                                                   |
| --------- | --------- | ---------- | --------------------------------------------------------- |
| $name     | string    |            | `name` attribute of the `input[type="file"]`              |
| $up_name  | string    |            | File name to save, without extension                      |
| $label    | string    | 'ファイル' | Item label used in the error messages                     |
| $required | boolean   | true       | `false`: no error when no file is selected                |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Upload;

$upload = new Upload();
$upload->setUploadDir($this->config['upload_dir']['path']);

if ($upload->upload('image', uniqid('i_'), 'Image')) {
    $data = $upload->getUploadedData('image');
} else {
    $errors = $upload->getErrorMessage('image');
}

// Optional file
$upload->upload('attachment', uniqid('a_'), 'Attachment', false);
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/upload/methods/getuploadeddata ---


# Upload::getUploadedData

> Get the information of the uploaded file

## Description

```php
public mixed Upload::getUploadedData( string $name )
```

Returns an array describing the file saved by [Upload::upload](./upload.mdx), or `null` when no file was saved for `$name`.

| Key      | Remarks                                                      |
| -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ |
| name     | Saved file name (`$up_name` + extension)                     |
| org_name | Original file name                                           |
| type     | MIME type                                                    |
| size     | File size (bytes)                                            |
| up_time  | Upload time (Unix timestamp)                                 |
| width    | Image width (images only)                                    |
| height   | Image height (images only)                                   |
| attr     | `width="..." height="..."` string (images only)              |

## Arguments

| Name  | Data type | Default | Remarks                                      |
| ----- | --------- | ------- | -------------------------------------------- |
| $name | string    |         | `name` attribute of the `input[type="file"]` |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Upload;

$upload = new Upload();
$upload->setUploadDir($this->config['upload_dir']['path']);

if ($upload->upload('image', uniqid('i_'))) {
    $data = $upload->getUploadedData('image');
    // $data['name'] -> "i_65f0c3a1b2c4d.jpg"
}
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/upload/methods/iserror ---


# Upload::isError

> Check whether any upload error occurred

## Description

```php
public boolean Upload::isError()
```

Returns `true` once an error has occurred in any [Upload::upload](./upload.mdx) call on this object. Before that it returns `null` (falsy).

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Upload;

$upload = new Upload();
$upload->setUploadDir($this->config['upload_dir']['path']);

$upload->upload('image1', uniqid('i_'), 'Image 1');
$upload->upload('image2', uniqid('i_'), 'Image 2');

if ($upload->isError()) {
    // At least one of the files failed
}
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/upload/methods/geterrormessage ---


# Upload::getErrorMessage

> Get the error messages of a file

## Description

```php
public mixed Upload::getErrorMessage( string $name )
```

Returns the error messages of the last [Upload::upload](./upload.mdx) call for `$name` as an array, or `null` when there is no error. The messages are defined in `file_error_message.php` (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)).

## Arguments

| Name  | Data type | Default | Remarks                                      |
| ----- | --------- | ------- | -------------------------------------------- |
| $name | string    |         | `name` attribute of the `input[type="file"]` |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Upload;

$upload = new Upload();
$upload->setUploadDir($this->config['upload_dir']['path']);

if (!$upload->upload('image', uniqid('i_'), 'Image')) {
    var_dump($upload->getErrorMessage('image'));
    // -> array('<strong>Image</strong> のサイズが大きすぎます')
}
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation ---


import { CategoryDocCardList } from '@site/src/components/CategoryDocCardList';

# Introduction

> Providing some functions for validation

## How to use

Import `Fegg\Validation` and create it with `new`. Call a check method for each field, then read the result with [Validation::getErrorMessage](./methods/geterrormessage.mdx).

```php title="Controller"
<?php
namespace Site\Controller;

defined('FEGG_CORE_DIR') || exit;

use Fegg\Validation;

class Contact extends Controller
{
    public function confirm()
    {
        $validation = new Validation();

        $id   = 'name';
        $name = 'お名前';
        $validation->required($id, $this->in($id), array('@required', $name));
        $validation->maxlength($id, $this->in($id), 50, array('@maxlength', $name, 50));

        $id   = 'mail';
        $name = 'メールアドレス';
        $validation->required($id, $this->in($id), array('@required', $name));
        $validation->email($id, $this->in($id), array('@email', $name));

        $errorMessage = $validation->getErrorMessage();
        if (!$errorMessage) {
            $this->displayPage('contact/confirm');
        } else {
            $this->page['error'] = $errorMessage;
            $this->displayPage('contact/input');
        }
    }
}
```

## How checks work

- Every check method takes the field name `$name` first. It is the key of the error message returned by [Validation::getErrorMessage](./methods/geterrormessage.mdx).
- Only the first error of each field is kept: once a field has an error, the next checks of the same field are skipped and return `false`. Write the checks in order of priority (e.g. `required` before `email`).
- Except [Validation::required](./methods/required.mdx) and [Validation::upload](./methods/upload.mdx), the checks do not check an empty value (they return `true`). Use `required` for required fields.
- `$code` decides the error message, see [Configuration](./configuration.mdx).

## Methods

<CategoryDocCardList label="Methods" />


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation/configuration ---


# Configuration

## Error messages

The error messages are defined in `htdocs/_fegg/core/config/validation_error_message.php`.

To change them, do not edit the core: copy the file to `htdocs/_fegg/site/config/validation_error_message.php` and edit the copy. The file in `site/config/` is used instead of the core's one (the whole file is replaced, so keep every key you use).

```php title="htdocs/_fegg/core/config/validation_error_message.php"
<?php
defined('FEGG_CORE_DIR') || exit;

/**
 * Error messages for the Validation class.
 *
 * @see Validation.php
 */
// @item should be Alphanumeric.
$validation_error_message['@alphameric'] = '<strong>%s</strong> は半角英数で入力してください';
// @item is not Date format.
$validation_error_message['@date'] = '<strong>%s</strong> の日付では登録できません';
// @item is not Email format.
$validation_error_message['@email'] = '<strong>%s</strong> は登録できない形式です';
// @item should be half size character.
$validation_error_message['@hankaku'] = '<strong>%s</strong> は半角で入力してください';
// @item should be KATAKANA.
$validation_error_message['@katakana'] = '<strong>%s</strong> はカタカナで入力してください';
// @item must be at most N characters long.
$validation_error_message['@maxlength'] = '<strong>%s</strong> は <strong>%s</strong> 文字以内で入力してください';
// @item must be at least N characters long.
$validation_error_message['@minlength'] = '<strong>%s</strong> は <strong>%s</strong> 文字以上で入力してください';
// @item should be Numeric.
$validation_error_message['@numeric'] = '<strong>%s</strong> は 半角数字で入力してください';
// @item should be Numeric to Integers or Decimals.
$validation_error_message['@numeric_decimalpoint'] = '<strong>%s</strong> は 半角の整数もしくは小数で入力してください';
// @item should be half size character to Numeric and Minus sign.
$validation_error_message['@numeric_minus'] = '<strong>%s</strong> は 数字、マイナス符号とも半角で入力してください';
// @item is unable character type.
$validation_error_message['@password'] = '<strong>%s</strong> は登録できない形式です';
// @item should be mixing of alphanumeric.
$validation_error_message['@password_mixed_letters'] = '<strong>%s</strong> は半角英数の混合で入力してください';
// @item is required.
$validation_error_message['@required'] = '<strong>%s</strong> をご入力ください';
// @item is not Tel format.
$validation_error_message['@tel'] = '<strong>%s</strong> は登録できない形式です';
// @item is not Time format.
$validation_error_message['@time'] = '<strong>%s</strong> の時間では登録できません';
// @item is not Zipcode format.
$validation_error_message['@zipcode'] = '<strong>%s</strong> は登録できない形式です';
```

There are no codes for [url](./methods/url.mdx), [userid](./methods/userid.mdx), [length](./methods/length.mdx) and [maxbyte](./methods/maxbyte.mdx). Pass the message itself, or add your own codes to the copy in `site/config/`.

## The `$code` argument

The `$code` argument of the check methods, [Validation::setError](./methods/seterror.mdx) and [Validation::upload](./methods/upload.mdx) can be:

| `$code` | Error message |
| --- | --- |
| `'@required'` | The message of the code as it is |
| `array('@required', 'Name')` | The message of the first element, with each `%s` replaced by the following elements in order (`vsprintf()`). An element that is itself a code (e.g. `'@foo'`) is replaced by its message |
| `'Please enter your name'` | A string that is not a code is used as the message |

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Validation;

$validation = new Validation();

$validation->maxlength('name', $this->in('name'), 50, array('@maxlength', 'お名前', 50));
// -> '<strong>お名前</strong> は <strong>50</strong> 文字以内で入力してください'

$validation->required('agree', $this->in('agree'), '<strong>利用規約</strong> に同意してください');
// -> '<strong>利用規約</strong> に同意してください'
```

## Other languages

When a language code is passed to the constructor (`new Validation('en')`), the messages are read from `config/en/validation_error_message.php` (searched in `site/config/` first, then `core/config/`). The core has no language files: create the file in `site/config/en/`.


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation/methods/construct ---


# Validation::\_\_construct

> Create the validator and load the error messages

## Description

```php
public Validation::__construct( [ string $languageCode = '' ] )
```

Loads the error messages from `validation_error_message.php` (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)). The file in `site/config/` is used when it exists, otherwise the core's one.

## Arguments

| Name          | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                                                    |
| ------------- | --------- | ------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| $languageCode | string    | ''      | Language code / if set, `config/{languageCode}/validation_error_message.php` is used (the file must exist) |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Validation;

$validation = new Validation();

// English error messages from config/en/validation_error_message.php
$validationEn = new Validation('en');
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation/methods/required ---


# Validation::required

> Check required

## Description

```php
public boolean Validation::required( string $name, mixed $value, [ mixed $code = '' ] )
```

A string is valid when it is not empty after trimming (`'0'` is valid). For an array, only the first element is checked: it must not be `''` or `null`.

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                                                      |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| $name  | string    |         | Field name (key of the error message)                                                                        |
| $value | mixed     |         | Value to check                                                                                               |
| $code  | mixed     | ''      | Error code, array of error code and parameters, or error message (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)) |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Validation;

$validation = new Validation();

$validation->required('name', 'John', array('@required', 'Name')); // -> true
$validation->required('mail', '', array('@required', 'Email'));    // -> false

var_dump($validation->isError()); // -> true
var_dump($validation->getErrorMessage());
// -> array('mail' => '<strong>Email</strong> をご入力ください')
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation/methods/alphameric ---


# Validation::alphameric

> Check half-width alphanumeric characters

## Description

```php
public boolean Validation::alphameric( string $name, mixed $value, [ mixed $code = '' ] )
```

Valid when the value contains only `0-9`, `a-z` and `A-Z`.

An empty value (`''`, `null`, `'0'`, whitespace only) is not checked and returns `true`: combine with [Validation::required](./required.mdx) when the field is required. When `$name` already has an error, the check is skipped and returns `false`, so only the first error of each field is kept.

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                                                      |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| $name  | string    |         | Field name (key of the error message)                                                                        |
| $value | mixed     |         | Value to check                                                                                               |
| $code  | mixed     | ''      | Error code, array of error code and parameters, or error message (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)) |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Validation;

$validation = new Validation();

$validation->alphameric('success', 'abc123', array('@alphameric', 'Code')); // -> true
$validation->alphameric('failed', 'abc-123', array('@alphameric', 'Code')); // -> false

var_dump($validation->getErrorMessage());
// -> array('failed' => '<strong>Code</strong> は半角英数で入力してください')
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation/methods/numeric ---


# Validation::numeric

> Check numeric or not

## Description

```php
public boolean Validation::numeric( string $name, mixed $value, [ mixed $code = '', boolean $decimalPointFlag = false, boolean $minusFlag = false ] )
```

Valid when the value contains only the digits `0-9`. `$decimalPointFlag` also allows a decimal part (`12.5`) and `$minusFlag` a leading `-`. Use the error codes `@numeric_decimalpoint` and `@numeric_minus` for the messages of those cases.

An empty value (`''`, `null`, `'0'`, whitespace only) is not checked and returns `true`: combine with [Validation::required](./required.mdx) when the field is required. When `$name` already has an error, the check is skipped and returns `false`, so only the first error of each field is kept.

## Arguments

| Name              | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                                                      |
| ----------------- | --------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| $name             | string    |         | Field name (key of the error message)                                                                        |
| $value            | mixed     |         | Value to check                                                                                               |
| $code             | mixed     | ''      | Error code, array of error code and parameters, or error message (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)) |
| $decimalPointFlag | boolean   | false   | `true`: allow a decimal part                                                                                 |
| $minusFlag        | boolean   | false   | `true`: allow a leading `-`                                                                                  |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Validation;

$validation = new Validation();

$validation->numeric('success', '123', array('@numeric', 'Quantity')); // -> true
$validation->numeric('failed', '12.5', array('@numeric', 'Quantity')); // -> false

var_dump($validation->getErrorMessage());
// -> array('failed' => '<strong>Quantity</strong> は 半角数字で入力してください')

// Decimals
$validation->numeric('price', '12.5', array('@numeric_decimalpoint', 'Price'), true); // -> true

// Negative numbers
$validation->numeric('offset', '-10', array('@numeric_minus', 'Offset'), false, true); // -> true
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation/methods/date ---


# Validation::date

> Check date format

## Description

```php
public boolean Validation::date( string $name, mixed $value, [ mixed $code = '' ] )
```

The separators `/` and `-` are removed, then the value must be digits only: `YYYY` (a year), `YYYYMM` (a year and a month from 01 to 12) or `YYYYMMDD` (an existing date, checked with `checkdate()`). So `2024`, `2024/04`, `2024-04-01` and `20240401` are valid; `2024/4/1` is not (8 digits are required for a day).

An empty value (`''`, `null`, `'0'`, whitespace only) is not checked and returns `true`: combine with [Validation::required](./required.mdx) when the field is required. When `$name` already has an error, the check is skipped and returns `false`, so only the first error of each field is kept.

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                                                      |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| $name  | string    |         | Field name (key of the error message)                                                                        |
| $value | mixed     |         | Value to check                                                                                               |
| $code  | mixed     | ''      | Error code, array of error code and parameters, or error message (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)) |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Validation;

$validation = new Validation();

$validation->date('success', '2024-04-01', array('@date', 'Date')); // -> true
$validation->date('failed', '2024-02-30', array('@date', 'Date')); // -> false

var_dump($validation->getErrorMessage());
// -> array('failed' => '<strong>Date</strong> の日付では登録できません')
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation/methods/email ---


# Validation::email

> Check email address format

## Description

```php
public boolean Validation::email( string $name, mixed $value, [ mixed $code = '' ] )
```

Valid when the value looks like an email address (`local@domain.tld`, the top level domain having 2 letters or more).

An empty value (`''`, `null`, `'0'`, whitespace only) is not checked and returns `true`: combine with [Validation::required](./required.mdx) when the field is required. When `$name` already has an error, the check is skipped and returns `false`, so only the first error of each field is kept.

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                                                      |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| $name  | string    |         | Field name (key of the error message)                                                                        |
| $value | mixed     |         | Value to check                                                                                               |
| $code  | mixed     | ''      | Error code, array of error code and parameters, or error message (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)) |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Validation;

$validation = new Validation();

$validation->email('success', 'foo@example.com', array('@email', 'Email')); // -> true
$validation->email('failed', 'foo@example', array('@email', 'Email')); // -> false

var_dump($validation->getErrorMessage());
// -> array('failed' => '<strong>Email</strong> は登録できない形式です')
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation/methods/tel ---


# Validation::tel

> Check telephone number format

## Description

```php
public boolean Validation::tel( string $name, mixed $value, [ mixed $code = '' ] )
```

Valid when the value contains only digits, `-`, `(` and `)`, optionally starts with `+` (country code), and ends with a digit. For example `03-1234-5678`, `(03)1234-5678` and `+81-3-1234-5678`.

An empty value (`''`, `null`, `'0'`, whitespace only) is not checked and returns `true`: combine with [Validation::required](./required.mdx) when the field is required. When `$name` already has an error, the check is skipped and returns `false`, so only the first error of each field is kept.

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                                                      |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| $name  | string    |         | Field name (key of the error message)                                                                        |
| $value | mixed     |         | Value to check                                                                                               |
| $code  | mixed     | ''      | Error code, array of error code and parameters, or error message (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)) |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Validation;

$validation = new Validation();

$validation->tel('success', '03-1234-5678', array('@tel', 'Tel')); // -> true
$validation->tel('failed', '03 1234 5678', array('@tel', 'Tel')); // -> false

var_dump($validation->getErrorMessage());
// -> array('failed' => '<strong>Tel</strong> は登録できない形式です')
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation/methods/time ---


# Validation::time

> Check time format

## Description

```php
public boolean Validation::time( string $name, mixed $value, [ mixed $code = '' ] )
```

The separator `:` is removed, then the value must be 4 digits `HHMM` in 24-hour format (`00:00` to `23:59`). `9:30` is not valid: write `09:30`.

An empty value (`''`, `null`, `'0'`, whitespace only) is not checked and returns `true`: combine with [Validation::required](./required.mdx) when the field is required. When `$name` already has an error, the check is skipped and returns `false`, so only the first error of each field is kept.

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                                                      |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| $name  | string    |         | Field name (key of the error message)                                                                        |
| $value | mixed     |         | Value to check                                                                                               |
| $code  | mixed     | ''      | Error code, array of error code and parameters, or error message (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)) |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Validation;

$validation = new Validation();

$validation->time('success', '09:30', array('@time', 'Time')); // -> true
$validation->time('failed', '24:00', array('@time', 'Time')); // -> false

var_dump($validation->getErrorMessage());
// -> array('failed' => '<strong>Time</strong> の時間では登録できません')
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation/methods/url ---


# Validation::url

> Check URL format

## Description

```php
public boolean Validation::url( string $name, mixed $value, [ mixed $code = '' ] )
```

Valid when the value starts with `http://`, `https://` or `ftp://` and contains only the characters allowed in a URL.

An empty value (`''`, `null`, `'0'`, whitespace only) is not checked and returns `true`: combine with [Validation::required](./required.mdx) when the field is required. When `$name` already has an error, the check is skipped and returns `false`, so only the first error of each field is kept.

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                                                      |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| $name  | string    |         | Field name (key of the error message)                                                                        |
| $value | mixed     |         | Value to check                                                                                               |
| $code  | mixed     | ''      | Error code, array of error code and parameters, or error message (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)) |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Validation;

$validation = new Validation();

$validation->url('success', 'https://example.com/', '<strong>URL</strong> の形式が正しくありません'); // -> true
$validation->url('failed', 'javascript:alert(1)', '<strong>URL</strong> の形式が正しくありません'); // -> false

var_dump($validation->getErrorMessage());
// -> array('failed' => '<strong>URL</strong> の形式が正しくありません')
```

:::note

The default `validation_error_message.php` has no `@url` code. Pass the error message itself, or add the code to your `site/config/validation_error_message.php`.

:::


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation/methods/zipcode ---


# Validation::zipcode

> Check Japanese postal code format

## Description

```php
public boolean Validation::zipcode( string $name, mixed $value, [ mixed $code = '' ] )
```

Valid when the value is 3 digits and 4 digits, with or without `-` between them (`123-4567` or `1234567`).

An empty value (`''`, `null`, `'0'`, whitespace only) is not checked and returns `true`: combine with [Validation::required](./required.mdx) when the field is required. When `$name` already has an error, the check is skipped and returns `false`, so only the first error of each field is kept.

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                                                      |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| $name  | string    |         | Field name (key of the error message)                                                                        |
| $value | mixed     |         | Value to check                                                                                               |
| $code  | mixed     | ''      | Error code, array of error code and parameters, or error message (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)) |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Validation;

$validation = new Validation();

$validation->zipcode('success', '123-4567', array('@zipcode', 'Zip code')); // -> true
$validation->zipcode('failed', '123-456', array('@zipcode', 'Zip code')); // -> false

var_dump($validation->getErrorMessage());
// -> array('failed' => '<strong>Zip code</strong> は登録できない形式です')
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation/methods/userid ---


# Validation::userid

> Check user ID format

## Description

```php
public boolean Validation::userid( string $name, mixed $value, [ mixed $code = '' ] )
```

Valid when the value contains only `0-9`, `a-z`, `A-Z`, `-`, `_` and `.`.

An empty value (`''`, `null`, `'0'`, whitespace only) is not checked and returns `true`: combine with [Validation::required](./required.mdx) when the field is required. When `$name` already has an error, the check is skipped and returns `false`, so only the first error of each field is kept.

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                                                      |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| $name  | string    |         | Field name (key of the error message)                                                                        |
| $value | mixed     |         | Value to check                                                                                               |
| $code  | mixed     | ''      | Error code, array of error code and parameters, or error message (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)) |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Validation;

$validation = new Validation();

$validation->userid('success', 'john.doe_01', '<strong>User ID</strong> は登録できない形式です'); // -> true
$validation->userid('failed', 'john doe', '<strong>User ID</strong> は登録できない形式です'); // -> false

var_dump($validation->getErrorMessage());
// -> array('failed' => '<strong>User ID</strong> は登録できない形式です')
```

:::note

The default `validation_error_message.php` has no `@userid` code. Pass the error message itself, or add the code to your `site/config/validation_error_message.php`.

:::


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation/methods/password ---


# Validation::password

> Check password format

## Description

```php
public boolean Validation::password( string $name, mixed $value, [ mixed $code = '', boolean $mixedLettersFlag = false ] )
```

Valid when the value contains only `0-9`, `a-z`, `A-Z` and `-_@!#`. With `$mixedLettersFlag` it must also contain at least one letter and one digit; use the error code `@password_mixed_letters` for that message. The length is not checked: combine with [Validation::minlength](./minlength.mdx).

An empty value (`''`, `null`, `'0'`, whitespace only) is not checked and returns `true`: combine with [Validation::required](./required.mdx) when the field is required. When `$name` already has an error, the check is skipped and returns `false`, so only the first error of each field is kept.

## Arguments

| Name              | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                                                      |
| ----------------- | --------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| $name             | string    |         | Field name (key of the error message)                                                                        |
| $value            | mixed     |         | Value to check                                                                                               |
| $code             | mixed     | ''      | Error code, array of error code and parameters, or error message (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)) |
| $mixedLettersFlag | boolean   | false   | `true`: require both letters and digits                                                                      |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Validation;

$validation = new Validation();

$validation->password('success', 'secret_123', array('@password', 'Password')); // -> true
$validation->password('failed', 'pass word', array('@password', 'Password')); // -> false

var_dump($validation->getErrorMessage());
// -> array('failed' => '<strong>Password</strong> は登録できない形式です')

// Letters and digits are both required
$validation->password('password2', 'secret', array('@password_mixed_letters', 'Password'), true); // -> false
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation/methods/maxbyte ---


# Validation::maxbyte

> Check maximum byte length

## Description

```php
public boolean Validation::maxbyte( string $name, mixed $value, numeric $byte, [ mixed $code = '' ] )
```

Valid when the byte length (`strlen()`) of the value is `$byte` or less. A Japanese character is 3 bytes in UTF-8.

An empty value (`''`, `null`, `'0'`, whitespace only) is not checked and returns `true`: combine with [Validation::required](./required.mdx) when the field is required. When `$name` already has an error, the check is skipped and returns `false`, so only the first error of each field is kept.

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                                                      |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| $name  | string    |         | Field name (key of the error message)                                                                        |
| $value | mixed     |         | Value to check                                                                                               |
| $byte  | numeric   |         | Maximum number of bytes                                                                                      |
| $code  | mixed     | ''      | Error code, array of error code and parameters, or error message (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)) |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Validation;

$validation = new Validation();

$validation->maxbyte('success', 'abc', 5, '<strong>Name</strong> が長すぎます'); // -> true
$validation->maxbyte('failed', 'あいう', 5, '<strong>Name</strong> が長すぎます'); // -> false

var_dump($validation->getErrorMessage());
// -> array('failed' => '<strong>Name</strong> が長すぎます')
```

:::note

The default `validation_error_message.php` has no `@maxbyte` code. Pass the error message itself, or add the code to your `site/config/validation_error_message.php`.

:::


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation/methods/length ---


# Validation::length

> Check exact character length

## Description

```php
public boolean Validation::length( string $name, mixed $value, numeric $length, [ mixed $code = '' ] )
```

Valid when the number of characters (`mb_strlen()` in UTF-8) is exactly `$length`.

An empty value (`''`, `null`, `'0'`, whitespace only) is not checked and returns `true`: combine with [Validation::required](./required.mdx) when the field is required. When `$name` already has an error, the check is skipped and returns `false`, so only the first error of each field is kept.

## Arguments

| Name    | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                                                      |
| ------- | --------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| $name   | string    |         | Field name (key of the error message)                                                                        |
| $value  | mixed     |         | Value to check                                                                                               |
| $length | numeric   |         | Number of characters                                                                                         |
| $code   | mixed     | ''      | Error code, array of error code and parameters, or error message (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)) |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Validation;

$validation = new Validation();

$validation->length('success', '1234', 4, '<strong>PIN</strong> は4桁で入力してください'); // -> true
$validation->length('failed', '12345', 4, '<strong>PIN</strong> は4桁で入力してください'); // -> false

var_dump($validation->getErrorMessage());
// -> array('failed' => '<strong>PIN</strong> は4桁で入力してください')
```

:::note

The default `validation_error_message.php` has no `@length` code. Pass the error message itself, or add the code to your `site/config/validation_error_message.php`.

:::


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation/methods/maxlength ---


# Validation::maxlength

> Check maximum character length

## Description

```php
public boolean Validation::maxlength( string $name, mixed $value, numeric $length, [ mixed $code = '' ] )
```

Valid when the number of characters (`mb_strlen()` in UTF-8) is `$length` or less. The `@maxlength` message takes the label and the length as parameters.

An empty value (`''`, `null`, `'0'`, whitespace only) is not checked and returns `true`: combine with [Validation::required](./required.mdx) when the field is required. When `$name` already has an error, the check is skipped and returns `false`, so only the first error of each field is kept.

## Arguments

| Name    | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                                                      |
| ------- | --------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| $name   | string    |         | Field name (key of the error message)                                                                        |
| $value  | mixed     |         | Value to check                                                                                               |
| $length | numeric   |         | Maximum number of characters                                                                                 |
| $code   | mixed     | ''      | Error code, array of error code and parameters, or error message (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)) |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Validation;

$validation = new Validation();

$validation->maxlength('success', 'あいう', 5, array('@maxlength', 'Name', 5)); // -> true
$validation->maxlength('failed', 'あいうえおか', 5, array('@maxlength', 'Name', 5)); // -> false

var_dump($validation->getErrorMessage());
// -> array('failed' => '<strong>Name</strong> は <strong>5</strong> 文字以内で入力してください')
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation/methods/minlength ---


# Validation::minlength

> Check minimum character length

## Description

```php
public boolean Validation::minlength( string $name, mixed $value, numeric $length, [ mixed $code = '' ] )
```

Valid when the number of characters (`mb_strlen()` in UTF-8) is `$length` or more. The `@minlength` message takes the label and the length as parameters.

An empty value (`''`, `null`, `'0'`, whitespace only) is not checked and returns `true`: combine with [Validation::required](./required.mdx) when the field is required. When `$name` already has an error, the check is skipped and returns `false`, so only the first error of each field is kept.

## Arguments

| Name    | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                                                      |
| ------- | --------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| $name   | string    |         | Field name (key of the error message)                                                                        |
| $value  | mixed     |         | Value to check                                                                                               |
| $length | numeric   |         | Minimum number of characters                                                                                 |
| $code   | mixed     | ''      | Error code, array of error code and parameters, or error message (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)) |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Validation;

$validation = new Validation();

$validation->minlength('success', 'secret_123', 8, array('@minlength', 'Password', 8)); // -> true
$validation->minlength('failed', 'abc', 8, array('@minlength', 'Password', 8)); // -> false

var_dump($validation->getErrorMessage());
// -> array('failed' => '<strong>Password</strong> は <strong>8</strong> 文字以上で入力してください')
```

# Validation::hankaku

> Check half-width characters

## Description

```php
public boolean Validation::hankaku( string $name, mixed $value, [ mixed $code = '' ] )
```

Valid when the value contains no multi-byte character (the byte length equals the character length).

An empty value (`''`, `null`, `'0'`, whitespace only) is not checked and returns `true`: combine with [Validation::required](./required.mdx) when the field is required. When `$name` already has an error, the check is skipped and returns `false`, so only the first error of each field is kept.

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                                                      |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| $name  | string    |         | Field name (key of the error message)                                                                        |
| $value | mixed     |         | Value to check                                                                                               |
| $code  | mixed     | ''      | Error code, array of error code and parameters, or error message (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)) |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Validation;

$validation = new Validation();

$validation->hankaku('success', 'abc-123', array('@hankaku', 'Code')); // -> true
$validation->hankaku('failed', 'ａｂｃ', array('@hankaku', 'Code')); // -> false

var_dump($validation->getErrorMessage());
// -> array('failed' => '<strong>Code</strong> は半角で入力してください')
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation/methods/katakana ---


# Validation::katakana

> Check Katakana characters for Japanese language

## Description

```php
public boolean Validation::katakana( string $name, mixed $value, [ mixed $code = '', boolean $zenkakuFlag = false ] )
```

By default the value must contain only **half-width** Katakana and half-width symbols (`｡` to `ﾟ`), spaces, `(`, `)` and `.`. With `$zenkakuFlag` it must contain only **full-width** Katakana (`ァ`-`ヶ`), `ー` and full-width spaces.

An empty value (`''`, `null`, `'0'`, whitespace only) is not checked and returns `true`: combine with [Validation::required](./required.mdx) when the field is required. When `$name` already has an error, the check is skipped and returns `false`, so only the first error of each field is kept.

## Arguments

| Name         | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                                                      |
| ------------ | --------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| $name        | string    |         | Field name (key of the error message)                                                                        |
| $value       | mixed     |         | Value to check                                                                                               |
| $code        | mixed     | ''      | Error code, array of error code and parameters, or error message (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)) |
| $zenkakuFlag | boolean   | false   | `true`: full-width Katakana only / `false`: half-width Katakana only                                         |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Validation;

$validation = new Validation();

$validation->katakana('success', 'ﾃｽﾄ', array('@katakana', 'Kana')); // -> true
$validation->katakana('failed', 'テスト', array('@katakana', 'Kana')); // -> false

var_dump($validation->getErrorMessage());
// -> array('failed' => '<strong>Kana</strong> はカタカナで入力してください')

// Full-width Katakana
$validation->katakana('furi', 'テスト', array('@katakana', 'Furigana'), true); // -> true
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation/methods/upload ---


# Validation::upload

> Check that a file was uploaded

## Description

```php
public boolean Validation::upload( string $name, mixed $code )
```

Valid when `$_FILES[$name]` exists, has no upload error and is not empty. Unlike the other checks, it is not skipped when nothing was posted. To save the file and check its size and type, use [Upload](../../upload/index.mdx).

## Arguments

| Name  | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                          |
| ----- | --------- | ------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| $name | string    |         | `name` attribute of the `input[type="file"]` (also the key of the error message) |
| $code | mixed     |         | Error code, array of error code and parameters, or error message                 |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Validation;

$validation = new Validation();

$validation->upload('image', '<strong>Image</strong> を選択してください');

if ($validation->isError()) {
    // No file was uploaded
}
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation/methods/seterror ---


# Validation::setError

> Set an error by error code

## Description

```php
public mixed Validation::setError( string $name, [ mixed $code = '' ] )
```

Marks `$name` as an error with the message resolved from `$code` in the same way as the check methods. When `$name` already has an error, nothing changes and `false` is returned.

## Arguments

| Name  | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                                                      |
| ----- | --------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| $name | string    |         | Field name (key of the error message)                                                                        |
| $code | mixed     | ''      | Error code, array of error code and parameters, or error message (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)) |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Validation;

$validation = new Validation();

// Error code with parameters
$validation->setError('name', array('@required', 'Name'));

// Error message
$validation->setError('zip1', '<strong>Zip code</strong> をご入力ください');

var_dump($validation->getErrorMessage());
// -> array(
//     'name' => '<strong>Name</strong> をご入力ください',
//     'zip1' => '<strong>Zip code</strong> をご入力ください',
// )
```

:::note

`_setError()` takes the same arguments but replaces an existing error of `$name`. The contact form of the skeleton uses it for checks combining several fields.

:::


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation/methods/seterrormessage ---


# Validation::setErrorMessage

> Set an error message

## Description

```php
public void Validation::setErrorMessage( string $name, string $message )
```

Marks `$name` as an error with `$message` as it is. Error codes are not resolved, and an existing error of `$name` is replaced.

## Arguments

| Name     | Data type | Default | Remarks                               |
| -------- | --------- | ------- | ------------------------------------- |
| $name    | string    |         | Field name (key of the error message) |
| $message | string    |         | Error message                         |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Validation;

$validation = new Validation();

$validation->setErrorMessage('mail', 'This email address is already registered');

var_dump($validation->getErrorMessage());
// -> array('mail' => 'This email address is already registered')
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation/methods/iserror ---


# Validation::isError

> Check whether any error was set

## Description

```php
public boolean Validation::isError()
```

Returns `true` once any check failed or any error was set on this object.

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Validation;

$validation = new Validation();

$validation->required('name', 'John', array('@required', 'Name'));
var_dump($validation->isError()); // -> false

$validation->required('mail', '', array('@required', 'Email'));
var_dump($validation->isError()); // -> true
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation/methods/geterrormessage ---


# Validation::getErrorMessage

> Get error messages

## Description

```php
public mixed Validation::getErrorMessage()
```

Returns the error messages as an array of field name => message, or `false` when there is no error.

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Validation;

$validation = new Validation();

$validation->required('name', '', array('@required', 'Name'));
$validation->email('mail', 'foo@', array('@email', 'Email'));

$errorMessage = $validation->getErrorMessage();
// -> array(
//     'name' => '<strong>Name</strong> をご入力ください',
//     'mail' => '<strong>Email</strong> は登録できない形式です',
// )

if (!$errorMessage) {
    // No error
}
```
